Root data was generated before\\ngeneration_config fix (top_k=20, T=0.6), shards are post-fix (top_k=0, T=0.7).\\n\\n用法:\\n  cd /root/autodl-tmp && python -m freige.training.merge_shards \\\\\\n      --shard_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations \\\\\\n      --output /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/generations_merged.jsonl\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--shard_dir\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n                        help=\\\"包含 shard_0..shard_N 子目录 + 可选 top-level generations.jsonl 的目录\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n                        help=\\\"合并后的输出文件路径\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    shard_dir = Path(args.shard_dir)\\n    output_path = Path(args.output)\\n\\n    sources = []\\n\\n    # top-level generations.jsonl (早期单卡运行的输出)\\n    top_level = shard_dir / \\\"generations.jsonl\\\"\\n    if top_level.exists() and str(output_path.resolve()) != str(top_level.resolve()):\\n        sources.append(top_level)\\n\\n    # shard 子目录 (按编号排序)\\n    shard_dirs = sorted(shard_dir.glob(\\\"shard_*\\\"))\\n    for sd in shard_dirs:\\n        gen_file = sd / \\\"generations.jsonl\\\"\\n        if gen_file.exists():\\n            sources.append(gen_file)\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Found {len(sources)} source files:\\\")\\n\\n    # last-wins: shard 版本覆盖 root 版本\\n    seen = {}  # key: (doc_id, generation_idx) -> line\\n    overwritten = 0\\n\\n    for src in sources:\\n        n = 0\\n        src_overwritten = 0\\n        for line in open(src):\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line:\\n                continue\\n            rec = json.loads(line)\\n            key = (rec[\\\"doc_id\\\"], rec[\\\"generation_idx\\\"])\\n            if key in seen:\\n                src_overwritten += 1\\n                overwritten += 1\\n            seen[key] = line\\n            n += 1\\n        extra = f\\\" ({src_overwritten} overwriting earlier)\\\" if src_overwritten else \\\"\\\"\\n        print(f\\\"  {src}: {n} records{extra}\\\")\\n\\n    total_lines = len(seen)\\n    with open(output_path, \\\"w\\\") as fout:\\n        for line in seen.values():\\n            fout.write(line + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n\\n    unique_docs = len(set(k[0] for k in seen))\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nMerged: {total_lines} records, {unique_docs} unique docs\\\")\\n    if overwritten:\\n        print(f\\\"{overwritten} records overwritten by shard version\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Output: {output_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"RSFT Step 2: CED 打分 + 筛选。\\n\\n读取 rsft_generate.py 生成的 JSONL，对每个候选输出计算三种奖励:\\n  - format_reward: JSON 格式合规性\\n  - f1_reward: 与 gold triples 的 micro-F1\\n  - ced_reward: CED 对比证据判别奖励\\n\\n按组合阈值筛选高质量样本，输出 SFT 训练兼容格式。\\n\\n输入:\\n  - generations.jsonl (rsft_generate.py 的输出)\\n\\n输出 (保存到 --output_path):\\n  - rsft_train.jsonl: 筛选后的 SFT 训练数据 (JSONL)\\n  - rsft_scores.jsonl: 带分数的完整记录（供分析）\\n  - rsft_report.json: 统计报告\\n\\n依赖: transformers, torch, freige.rewards.ced_reward\\n\\n用法:\\n  python -m freige.training.rsft_score_filter \\\\\\n      --input_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations \\\\\\n      --output_path /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered \\\\\\n      --selection_strategy top_pct --top_pct 25 \\\\\\n      --nli_model_path /root/autodl-tmp/models/nli-deberta-v3-base\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"You are an information extraction model. Given a document with numbered sentences \\\"\\n    \\\"and a list of entities, extract all relation triples. For each triple, provide the \\\"\\n    \\\"head entity, relation type, tail entity, and the sentence IDs that serve as evidence. \\\"\\n    \\\"Output as a JSON list.\\\"\\n)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Reward functions — 复用 grpo_trainer 中的逻辑\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_format_reward(parsed_triples, format_ok):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"格式奖励，复用 grpo_trainer.format_reward_fn 的逻辑。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not format_ok:\\n        return 0.0\\n    if not parsed_triples:\\n        return 0.1\\n    valid = 0\\n    for item in parsed_triples:\\n        if (isinstance(item, dict) and \\\"head\\\" in item\\n                and \\\"relation\\\" in item and \\\"tail\\\" in item\\n                and \\\"evidence\\\" in item\\n                and isinstance(item[\\\"evidence\\\"], list)):\\n            valid += 1\\n    return valid / len(parsed_triples)\\n\\n\\ndef compute_f1_reward(parsed_triples, gold_triples):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"F1 奖励，复用 grpo_trainer.f1_reward_fn       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"RSFT (Rejection Sampling Fine-Tuning) 训练脚本。\\n\\n从 SFT warmup checkpoint 继续训练，使用经 CED 打分筛选的高质量生成数据。\\n流程: base model → merge SFT adapter → 新 QLoRA → 在 RSFT 数据上训练。\\n\\n输入: JSONL，每行 {\\\"doc_id\\\": ..., \\\"input\\\": ..., \\\"output\\\": ..., \\\"ced_score\\\": ..., ...}\\n输出: QLoRA adapter checkpoint\\n依赖: transformers, trl, peft, bitsandbytes, datasets\\n\\n单卡:   python -m freige.training.rsft_trainer --rsft_data_path data.jsonl --output_dir ./rsft_output\\n多卡:   accelerate launch --config_file configs/accelerate_4gpu.yaml \\\\\\n            -m freige.training.rsft_trainer --rsft_data_path data.jsonl --output_dir ./rsft_output\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom datasets import Dataset\\nfrom peft import LoraConfig, PeftModel, TaskType, get_peft_model\\nfrom torch.utils.data import WeightedRandomSampler\\nfrom transformers import (\\n    AutoModelForCausalLM,\\n    AutoTokenizer,\\n    BitsAndBytesConfig,\\n    TrainerCallback,\\n)\\nfrom trl import SFTConfig, SFTTrainer\\n\\nfrom freige.training.sft_trainer import SYSTEM_PROMPT\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nDEFAULT_BASE_MODEL = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B\\\"\\nDEFAULT_SFT_ADAPTER = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_rsft_data(data_path: str, tokenizer, max_length: int = 2048):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"加载 CED 筛选后的 RSFT 数据，格式化为 chat template 文本。\\n\\n    每行 JSONL 的 input 字段作为 user message，output 字段作为 assistant response，\\n    经 apply_chat_template 拼接后用于 SFT 训练。超过 max_length 的样本被跳过\\n    而非截断，避免训练在不完整 JSON 输出上。\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        (Dataset, list[float]): 训练数据集和对应的 CED scores\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    samples = []\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            if line.strip():\\n                samples.append(json.loads(line))\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loaded %d RSFT samples from %s\\\", len(samples), data_path)\\n\\n    formatted = []\\n    ced_scores = []\\n    skipped = 0\\n    for s in samples:\\n        messages = [\\n            {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\n            {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": s[\\\"input\\\"]},\\n            {\\\"role\\\": \\\"assistant\\\", \\\"content\\\": s[\\\"output\\\"]},\\n        ]\\n        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n            messages,\\n            tokenize=False,\\n            add_generation_prompt=False,\\n            enable_thinking=False,\\n        )\\n        token_len = len(tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False))\\n        if token_len > max_length:\\n            skipped += 1\\n            continue\\n        formatted.append({\\\"text\\\": text})\\n        ced_scores.append(s.get(\\\"ced_score\\\", 1.0))\\n\\n    if skipped:\\n        logger.warning(\\\"Skipped %d samples exceeding max_length=%d\\\", skipped, max_length)\\n\\n    if ced_scores:\\n        logger.info(\\n            \\\"Prepared %d RSFT samples (CED scores: min=%.3f, max=%.3f, mean=%.3f)\\\",\\n            len(formatted), min(ced_scores), max(ced_scores),\\n dev set for task-level evaluation during training.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from freige.data.docred_processor import DocREDProcessor\\n    from freige.eval.evaluator import gold_from_docred\\n\\n    processor = DocREDProcessor(data_dir=data_dir)\\n    samples = processor.process(\\\"dev\\\")\\n    groups = processor.group_by_document(samples)\\n\\n    eval_items = []\\n    for doc_id, doc_samples in list(groups.items())[:max_docs]:\\n        sft_sample = processor.format_sft_sample(doc_samples)\\n        messages = [\\n            {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\n            {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": f\\\"{sft_sample['instruction']}\\\\n\\\\n{sft_sample['input']}\\\"},\\n        ]\\n        prompt = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n            messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False,\\n        )\\n        eval_items.append({\\\"doc_id\\\": doc_id, Fresh LoRA for RSFT\\n    lora_config = LoraConfig(\\n        task_type=TaskType.CAUSAL_LM,\\n        r=args.lora_rank,\\n        lora_alpha=args.lora_alpha,\\n        lora_dropout=args.lora_dropout,\\n        target_modules=[\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\",\\n                         \\\"gate_proj\\\", \\\"up_proj\\\", \\\"down_proj\\\"],\\n    )\\n    model = get_peft_model(model, lora_config)\\n    model.print_trainable_parameters()\\n\\n    # Eval data\\n    eval_dataset = None\\n    callbacks = []\\n    if args.eval_data_dir:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Loading eval data from %s (max %d docs for task eval)\\\",\\n                     args.eval_data_dir, args.eval_max_docs)\\n        eval_items, gold, eval_dataset = prepare_eval_data(\\n            args.eval_data_dir, tokenizer, args.eval_max_docs,\\n        )\\n        train_file = os.path.join(args.eval_data_dir, \\\"train_annotated.json\\\")\\n        callbacks.append(TaskEvalCallback(\\n            tokenizer=tokenizer,\\n            eval_items=eval_items,\\n            gold=gold,\\n            train_file=train_file,\\n            max_new_tokens=args.max_new_tokens,\\n        ))\\n        logger.info(\\\"Task eval enabled: %d docs, %d gold triples\\\",\\n                     len(eval_items), len(gold))\\n\\n    # Training config\\n    training_args = SFTConfig(\\n        output_dir=args.output_dir,\\n        num_train_epochs=args.num_epochs,\\n        per_device_train_batch_size=args.per_device_batch_size,\\n        per_device_eval_batch_size=1,\\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=args.gradient_accumulation_steps,\\n        learning_rate=args.learning_rate,\\n        warmup_steps=args.warmup_steps,\\n        bf16=args.bf16,\\n        logging_steps=10,\\n        eval_strategy=\\\"epoch\\\" if eval_dataset is not None else \\\"no\\\",\\n        save_strategy=\\\"steps\\\",\\n        save_steps=args.save_steps,\\n        save_total_limit=args.save_total_limit,\\n        seed=args.seed,\\n        max_length=args.max_length,\\n        dataset_text_field=\\\"text\\\",\\n        report_to=\\\"wandb\\\" if \\\"WANDB_API_KEY\\\" in os.environ else \\\"none\\\",\\n        gradient_checkpointing=True,\\n        gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\\\"use_reentrant\\\": False},\\n        deepspeed=args.deepspeed,\\n        max_steps=args.max_steps,\\n    )\\n\\n    sample_weights = ced_scores if args.weighted_sampling else None\\n\\n    trainer = WeightedSFTTrainer(\\n        model=model,\\n        args=training_args,\\n        train_dataset=train_dataset,\\n        eval_dataset=eval_dataset,\\n        processing_class=tokenizer,\\n        sample_weights=sample_weights,\\n        callbacks=callbacks,\\n    )\\n\\n    logger.info(\\n        \\\"Starting RSFT training (%d samples, weighted=%s)...\\\",\\n        len(train_dataset), args.weighted_sampling,\\n    )\\n    trainer.train()\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Saving model to %s\\\", args.output_dir)\\n    trainer.save_model(args.output_dir)\\n    tokenizer.save_pretrained(args.output_dir)\\n    logger.info(\\\"RSFT training complete.\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.52}}"

range(n):\\n            negs = hard_negative_list[i]\\n            neg_counts.append(len(negs))\\n            all_neg_premises.extend(negs)\\n            all_neg_hyps.extend([claims[i]] * len(negs))\\n\\n        if all_neg_premises:\\n            all_neg_probs = self.nli_entailment_prob(all_neg_premises, all_neg_hyps)\\n        else:\\n            all_neg_probs = torch.tensor([])\\n\\n all_neg_probs[offset:offset + nc].max().item()\\n            else:\\n                p_neg = 0.0\\n            offset += nc\\n\\n            margin = p_pos - p_neg\\n            reward = max(0.0, margin) * float(p_pos > tau)\\n            results.append({\\n                \\\"reward\\\": reward,\\n                \\\"p_pos\\\": p_pos,\\n                \\\"p_neg\\\": p_neg,\\n 按 doc_id 分组，每组取 top-K\\n        by_doc = defaultdict(list)\\n        for rec in candidates:\\n            by_doc[rec[\\\"doc_id\\\"]].append(rec)\\n\\n        selected = []\\n        for doc_id, doc_recs in by_doc.items():\\n            doc_recs.sort(key=lambda x: x[\\\"combined_score\\\"], reverse=True)\\n            selected.extend(doc_recs[:args.top_k])\\n\\n        logger.info(\\\"After top_%d: %d records from %d documents\\\",\\n                     args.top_k, len(selected), len(by_doc))\\n        return selected\\n\\n    raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown selection strategy: {args.selection_strategy}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef format_sft_training_data(selected_records, tokenizer_name=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"将筛选后的记录转换为 rsft_trainer 期望的 JSONL 格式。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    sft_data = []\\n    for rec in selected_records:\\n        sft_data.append({\\n            \\\"input\\\": _extract_user_content(rec[\\\"prompt\\\"]),\\n            \\\"output\\\": rec[\\\"output\\\"],\\n            \\\"ced_score\\\": rec[\\\"ced_reward\\\"],\\n            \\\"doc_id\\\": rec[\\\"doc_id\\\"],\\n            \\\"generation_idx\\\": rec[\\\"generation_idx\\\"],\\n            \\\"format_reward\\\": rec[\\\"format_reward\\\"],\\n            \\\"f1_reward\\\": rec[\\\"f1_reward\\\"],\\n            \\\"combined_score\\\": rec[\\\"combined_score\\\"],\\n        })\\n\\n    return sft_data\\n\\n\\ndef _extract_user_content(prompt_text):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"从完整 prompt 中提取 user 消息内容。\\n\\n    prompt 是 apply_chat_template 的输出，包含 system/user 标记。\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # Qwen3 chat template 格式:\\n    # <|im_start|>system\\\\n...<|im_end|>\\\\n<|im_start|>user\\\\n...<|im_end|>\\\\n<|im_start|>assistant\\\\n\\n    user_start = prompt_text.find(\\\"<|im_start|>user\\\\n\\\")\\n    if user_start < 0:\\n        return prompt_text\\n    content_start = user_start + len(\\\"<|im_start|>user\\\\n\\\")\\n    user_end = prompt_text.find(\\\"<|im_end|>\\\", content_start)\\n    if user_end < 0:\\n        return prompt_text[content_start:]\\n    return prompt_text[content_start:user_end]\\n\\n\\ndef generate_report(scored_records, selected_records):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"生成统计报告。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    total = len(scored_records)\\n    passed = len(selected_records)\\n\\n    scores = {\\n        \\\"format_reward\\\": [r[\\\"format_reward\\\"] for r in scored_records],\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.53}}"
    }
  }
}

全部代码已审查完毕。汇报如下：

---

## 1. Merge 脚本

**`/root/autodl-tmp/freige/training/merge_shards.py` — 已存在，无需新写。**

用法：
```bash
cd /root/autodl-tmp && python -m freige.training.merge_shards \
    --shard_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations \
    --output /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/generations_merged.jsonl
```

去重策略：`(doc_id, generation_idx)` 为 key，last-wins（shard 覆盖 root）。会自动处理 shard_1 续传重复。

当前各 shard 行数：shard_0=5280, shard_1=5552, shard_2=5568, shard_3=5032, 总计 21432 行。

---

## 2. rsft_score_filter.py — 输入/输出/参数

**输入**: `--input_dir`（含 shard_* 子目录的目录），内部 `load_generations()` 自带合并 + 去重逻辑，所以可以跳过 merge_shards 直接指向 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations`。

**输出**（`--output_path` 目录下）：
- `rsft_train.jsonl` — 筛选后的 SFT 训练数据，字段：`{input, output, ced_score, doc_id, generation_idx, format_reward, f1_reward, combined_score}`
- `rsft_scores.jsonl` — 全量打分记录
- `rsft_report.json` — 统计报告

**关键参数**：
| 参数 | 默认值 | 说明 |
|------|--------|------|
| `--selection_strategy` | `top_pct` | threshold / top_k / top_pct |
| `--top_pct` | 25 | 每 doc 取 top 25% |
| `--format_threshold` | 0.5 | format_reward 硬阈值 |
| `--format_weight / f1_weight / ced_weight` | 0.2 / 0.4 / 0.4 | combined_score 权重 |
| `--nli_model_path` | `cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base` | NLI 模型 |
| `--nli_device` | auto | 可指定 `cuda:0` |
| `--tau` | 0.5 | CED 阈值 |

---

## 3. rsft_trainer.py — 配置和格式

**输入格式**：JSONL，每行 `{input, output, ced_score(optional)}`。与 score_filter 的输出格式**匹配**。

**关键配置**：
- 支持 bf16 + QLoRA（4-bit nf4）
- base model: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B`
- SFT adapter: `/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output`（先 merge SFT adapter 再加新 LoRA）
- LoRA: r=16, alpha=32, 7 个 target_modules
- 支持 `--weighted_sampling`（用 ced_score 做 WeightedRandomSampler）
- 支持 accelerate 多卡
- 内置 TaskEvalCallback（eval_data_dir 指向 DocRED dev 时可跑 task eval）

---

## 4. 数据格式对齐 — **有一个关键 bug**

**generation → score_filter 格式不匹配：**

generation JSONL 的字段：`doc_id, generation_idx, prompt, output(string), raw_text, format_ok, truncated, gold_triples, sents`

但 `score_all_records()` 直接访问 `rec["parsed_triples"]`（第 ~280 行），这个字段在 generation 输出中**不存在**。`output` 是 JSON 字符串，不是已解析的 list。

**这会导致 `KeyError: 'parsed_triples'` 崩溃。**

修复方案：在 `load_generations()` 末尾或 `score_all_records()` 开头，对每条记录做：
```python
if "parsed_triples" not in rec:
    try:
        rec["parsed_triples"] = json.loads(rec["output"])
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        rec["parsed_triples"] = []
```

score_filter → trainer 格式：`format_sft_training_data()` 输出 `{input, output, ced_score, ...}`，trainer 的 `load_rsft_data()` 读取 `s["input"]` 和 `s["output"]`。**格式对齐，无问题。**

---

## 5. CED 筛选资源需求

- NLI 模型：DeBERTa-v3-base，磁盘 715MB，VRAM **< 1GB**（fp32）
- **1 卡即可**，其余 3 卡完全空闲
- 预计 21K records × ~10 triples/record = ~210K NLI 推理，DeBERTa 在 RTX 5090 上单推理 < 1ms，估计 **15-30 分钟**完成全部打分

---

## 6. 需要修复的问题

| 优先级 | 问题 | 修复 |
|--------|------|------|
| **P0** | `rsft_score_filter.py` 缺少 `output` → `parsed_triples` 的解析步骤 | 在 `load_generations` 或 `score_all_records` 中添加 `json.loads(rec["output"])` |
| P2 | `--nli_model_path` 默认值是 HF hub ID，不是本地路径。本地缓存路径是 `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/hub/models--cross-encoder--nli-deberta-v3-base/snapshots/...` | 运行时用 `--nli_model_path` 指定本地路径，或设 `HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache` |

P0 bug 不修复的话 score_filter 跑不起来。需要我现在修复吗？
